| **STEP B — DEC-066 §(h) resolution (documented investigation; NOT deferred to fetcher commit)** | **Live read-only probe of `public.signal_observations` constraints** (run 2026-06-23 against the live external Supabase backend via `supabase--read_query`; the repo migration `supabase/migrations/20260605113546_…sql` is authoritative for the original DDL, but the live constraint set is what the fetcher hits): <br/><br/> **Columns (`information_schema.columns`):** `operator_id uuid NOT NULL`, `signal_id text NOT NULL`, `as_of_date date NOT NULL`, `ticker text NOT NULL`, `value double precision NULL`, `is_present boolean NOT NULL`, `gics_sector text NULL`, `computed_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()`, `carried_forward boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false`. <br/><br/> **Constraints (`pg_constraint`):** (1) `signal_observations_pkey` = `PRIMARY KEY (operator_id, signal_id, as_of_date, ticker)`. (2) `signal_observations_value_is_present_check` = `CHECK ((value IS NULL AND is_present = false) OR (value IS NOT NULL AND is_present = true))`. (3) `signal_observations_carried_forward_present_check` = `CHECK ((carried_forward = false) OR (is_present = true AND value IS NOT NULL))`. <br/><br/> **DDL-vs-live reconciliation:** the repo migration shows columns + constraints (1) + (2); the live DB additionally carries `carried_forward boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false` + constraint (3). This is the DEC-060 short-interest-carry-forward additive landing, NOT a divergence — both the column and the CHECK are additive and consistent with the repo's later migration trail. No constraint exists on `signal_observations` that the repo did not introduce. <br/><br/> **Admissibility verdict for a market-level row:** (a) `signal_id` is free-text with NO value-constraining CHECK / enum — a new `market_24m_cumulative_return` / `market_realized_vol_6m` `signal_id` is admissible with NO widening. (b) `ticker` is NOT NULL — a market-level row therefore needs a SENTINEL TICKER (NULL is rejected by the column constraint). (c) `value IS NOT NULL` + `is_present = true` for regime features (always-present under DEC-066 §(e) fail-loud) satisfies CHECK (2) AND CHECK (3) with `carried_forward = false`. (d) The PK admits one row per `(operator_id, market_signal_id, as_of_date, sentinel_ticker)` — exactly the per-`as_of_date` cardinality DEC-066 §(b) specifies. <br/><br/> **CONCLUSION: NO CHECK widening, NO migration on `signal_observations` or `combiner_feature_vectors` needed. Gate-3.2 "without migration" holds.** The only DDL deltas in the 3.2-b..3.2-e ladder are additive new rows (cron + `signal_registry`) on existing tables, not schema changes on the feature carriers. <br/><br/> **Sentinel-ticker convention chosen: `__MARKET__`** (literal string, double-underscore prefix + suffix). <br/><br/> **Rationale (collision-checked against the existing assembler + signal readers):** (i) Per-name assembly intersects `signal_observations` with `universe_membership` and DROPS non-universe tickers (verified at `supabase/functions/_shared/longshort-combiner/shadow-ranker-orchestrator_test.ts` test `(sorch-5) non-universe tickers in signal_observations are dropped`); `__MARKET__` is not a real ticker and will never be in `universe_membership`, so it is naturally invisible to the per-name pass — the 3.2-c regime-broadcaster will read regime rows by `signal_id LIKE 'market\_%'` on a SEPARATE projection, not by ticker. No collision with existing ticker-filtering logic. (ii) Rejected alternative `SPY`: SPY is a real ticker (the Polygon source ticker for the fetcher itself); using it as the sentinel would conflate the input ticker with the broadcast-target sentinel and would falsely match `universe_membership` if SPY were ever admitted to the universe (the universe excludes ETFs today but that is not a contractual guarantee — DEC-054 R7 gross-scaling could shift this). (iii) Rejected alternative `NULL`: rejected by the `ticker text NOT NULL` column constraint per the live probe above. (iv) Double-underscore prefix + suffix is not a valid US-equity ticker character pattern per Polygon / SEC conventions — guaranteed not to collide with any real ticker now or in the future. (v) Visually unambiguous as a sentinel in diagnostic SELECT output; sorts to the top under `ORDER BY ticker ASC`. <br/><br/> **AUTHORING-TIME CHECK named in DEC-066 §(i) — RESOLVED here (3.2-a), NOT deferred to 3.2-b.** The 3.2-b fetcher prompt INHERITS this convention as a settled input + cites this FP entry as the governance trail. |
| **Sub-ladder (build sub-steps gated on this FP umbrella)** | **3.2-a — THIS** (FP entry + DEC-066 §(h) constraint resolution + sentinel-ticker convention `__MARKET__` chosen + logged). **3.2-b** — SPY regime fetcher edge function + new cron `longshort.spy_regime.compute` (seeded DISARMED) + `signal_registry` rows for the two `market_*` signals + `REGIME_PRICE_HISTORY_LOOKBACK_DAYS = 730` constant + typed-fail-loud per DEC-066 §(e); writes to `signal_observations` with `ticker='__MARKET__'`, `is_present=true`, `carried_forward=false`. NO `FEATURE_ORDER` change in 3.2-b — the assembler does not yet read the regime rows. **3.2-c** — assembler regime-broadcaster: read `signal_observations WHERE signal_id IN ('market_24m_cumulative_return','market_realized_vol_6m') AND as_of_date = $as_of` (NO universe intersection on this projection); broadcast the values into every per-name `combiner_feature_vectors.features` jsonb at that `as_of_date` under stable keys; typed-fail-loud propagates to the assemble outcome. NO `FEATURE_ORDER` change — the keys land in the jsonb carrier, indices flip in 3.2-d. **3.2-d** — paired TS+Python `FEATURE_ORDER` 16→18 (`lgbm-inference.ts` + `training/combiner/feature_contract.py`) + `EXPECTED_FEATURE_KEY_COUNT` 16→18 in `signal-catalog.ts`, SAME PR per DEC-066 §(d); `assert_feature_order_parity.py` re-run; `feature_order_hash` flips (ADDITIVE-NOT-BREAK at change time per DEC-066 §(f) — zero artifacts exist; POST-PROMOTION LOCK takes effect once the first artifact promotes). **3.2-e** — trainer re-validation: contract-shape check only (parity assert + trainer dry-run skip-gate; no fit because T+10 labels haven't accrued per ACT-288); confirms `feature_contract.py` `feature_order_hash()` output is byte-identical to the new TS hash. Gate-3.2 closes when 3.2-d + 3.2-e land green. |

Both handlers carry three deterministic skip gates in order BEFORE the orchestrator call, each emitting `.skipped` with a typed reason and performing NO write: (1) global kill-switch (`job_registry.__kill_switch__` row `enabled=false` → `reason='global_kill_switch_active'`); (2) own-row disarmed (`enabled=false` → `reason='job_disarmed'`); and on the RANK fn ONLY (3) **assemble-completion gate** — queries `longshort_audit_logs` for a same-`as_of_date` `.completed` row (cron OR manual variant) and fails closed if absent or on query error → `reason='assemble_incomplete_for_as_of'`. RATIONALE for the gate: the ranker's only input guard is `vectors_read === 0` (no partial-set guard); without the per-as_of structural check, a rank fire racing an in-progress assemble would silently produce a live book on a truncated universe. The 15-min schedule gap is only common-case timing. | **In-scope (3.0)** | (1) Five-table schema set under `public.combiner_*`: `combiner_feature_vectors` (jsonb-features, per-(operator_id, as_of_date, ticker)); `combiner_rankings` (PK `(operator_id, as_of_date, ticker)` + `long_score numeric NOT NULL` + `short_score numeric NOT NULL` + `long_rank int NOT NULL` + `short_rank int NOT NULL` + `ranker_source text NOT NULL` (literal `'count_normalized_fallback'` at 3.0a) + `gics_sector text` + `computed_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()` — NO `model_version_*` columns at 3.0a; 3.2 adds NULLable per-side `model_version_long` / `model_version_short` for independent per-side LightGBM model tracking with zero schema-change otherwise. Two-ranking shape per CROSSWIND §1.4 (L105/107/112/114/119) + §6.1/§6.2 (two independent LightGBM models): a name carries `long_rank` AND `short_rank` simultaneously on the same `as_of` (e.g. rank 18 long AND rank 412 short); book engine reads `long_rank ≤ 20` / `short_rank ≤ 20` / `> 30` / `21..30` directly per §1.4. Reconciled at ACT-232 per R1 — supersedes the prior single-`(side, score, rank_within_side)` shape from the FP-052 authoring commit `31e3a134` (REFUTED against the spec under dual independent investigation)); `combiner_book` (UNIQUE(operator_id, as_of_date, side, rank_within_side)); `combiner_model_registry` (per-side; `status text NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('active','candidate','retired'))` — matches `signal_registry.status` precedent (text + CHECK; NO new enum type — convention-aligned per ACT-232 R3a, supersedes the prior `combiner_model_status` enum proposal from `31e3a134`) + PARTIAL UNIQUE INDEX on `(side) WHERE status='active'` enforcing single-active invariant); `combiner_shap_attribution` (per-ranking, jsonb-attributions). RLS-first per D1; `service_role` write + `longshort.view` SELECT (canonical permission key per `docs/07-reference/permission-index.md` L659; the prior `longshort.read` reference from `31e3a134` was a F1 typo corrected at ACT-232 R2 — no `longshort.read` key exists in the permission registry). [3.0a schema apply LANDED at ACT-233 / MIG-099 — see ART-025; the 3.0a sub-phase is the migration commit, items (2)–(10) below remain 3.0b–d build scope.] (2) **TYPED-ABSENCE feature assembler** under `_shared/longshort-combiner/feature-assembler.ts` (3.0b) consuming `signal_observations` for the 9 live signals; emits per-name `combiner_feature_vectors.features` jsonb = {critical (#6, #7): bare numeric z-score; non-critical (#1/#2/#3/#4/#5/#8/#9): per-signal `{__value: <z-score>\|null, __is_present: 0\|1}`}; enforces §4.3.5 critical-exclusion gate (names missing Signal #6 cross_sectional_momentum_12_1 OR Signal #7 short_term_reversal_1w are EXCLUDED before fallback formula); §4.3.5 coverage gate (>4 of 7 non-critical missing → excluded; minimum 5 of 9 per L402 = 2 critical + 3 non-critical); excluded names persisted with `features={}` + `excluded_reason ∈ {missing_critical_signal_6, missing_critical_signal_7, below_coverage_threshold}` (verbatim MIG-099 `combiner_feature_vectors.excluded_reason` CHECK values; corrected from prior `insufficient_coverage` draft at ACT-235 — see `supabase/functions/_shared/longshort-combiner/signal-catalog.ts` `EXCLUDED_REASON` constants) + `coverage_count` for audit queryability (ranker filters `WHERE excluded_reason IS NULL`); GICS sector derived from first non-null `signal_observations.sector` per (ticker, as_of_date). **NO `Decimal('-999')` written to disk at 3.0b, NO `// allow-sentinel-fallback` annotation, NO `docs/banned-patterns.md` override entry, NO `check-sentinel-patterns_test.ts` single-site pinning** — all four repositioned to 3.2 per ACT-234 R1 typed-absence reconciliation. Reconciliation evidence (dual independent investigation Claude + Lovable against HEAD `1b23b44d`): §6.5.3 L649–651 ("the sentinel is introduced at exactly one place: the feature-vector construction function that assembles the 16-feature input to the combiner. All other code paths use `Optional[Decimal]`") — DB persistence is an "other code path" → typed-absence; §6.5.4 L657–662 (training samples include `value_i = Decimal('-999')` but the missingness profile is "captured per Phase 2 sub-phase and replayed at Phase 3 combiner training") → sentinel is re-introduced at train-time from typed-absence + profile, NOT persisted in the feature store; §2/L387 axiom 3 (`None` canonical absence; synthetic numerics on disk = SENTINEL defect class absent necessity-narrowed ADR exception — and no necessity exists since `__is_present=0` carries absence and -999 is bit-exactly reconstructible from the locked constant); §6.4 consumer reality — fallback formula `Σ(z_i × is_present_i) / max(1, Σ is_present_i)` reads `__is_present` and NEVER reads -999; only the 3.2 LightGBM model input reads -999. The single `Decimal('-999')` construction site therefore relocates to **the 3.2 in-process model-input construction function** (immediately before LightGBM `.predict()`; exact path resolved at 3.2 build PR) per **ADR-008a (supersedes ADR-008)**. (3) Fallback ranker under `_shared/longshort-combiner/count-normalized-fallback-ranker.ts` (3.0c) implementing §6.4 verbatim; writes `combiner_rankings.ranker_source='count_normalized_fallback'` on every row. The §6.4 formula `Σ(z_i × is_present_i) / max(1, Σ is_present_i)` reads typed-absence via `__is_present` (absent signals contribute zero to BOTH numerator and denominator — never reads `Decimal('-999')`), confirming the item-(2) typed-absence persistence is consumer-compatible with no downstream change required (ACT-234 R1). (4) Book builder selecting top-20-long / bottom-20-short per DEC-054 baseline; writes `combiner_book` with UNIQUE rank invariant; `combiner_book.rank_within_side` is sourced from the corresponding `combiner_rankings` row's `long_rank` (when `side='long'`) or `short_rank` (when `side='short'`) — explicit derivation eliminates the side-ambiguity at apply-time that the single-`rank_within_side` shape would otherwise re-introduce (ACT-232 R2 third stale item). (5) Idempotent persistence per T8: rankings + book write `ON CONFLICT (operator_id, as_of_date, ticker [, side, rank_within_side]) DO UPDATE` for deterministic replay. (6) Daily-post-signal cron cadence (union of signal crons; per-tick §6.4 cadence DEFERRED to Phase 5 live-trading per disposition (a)). (7) Edge function `longshort-combiner-compute` via DEC-023 envelope (T7); strategy-audit per T4 to `longshort_audit_logs` events `longshort.combiner.compute.{started,completed,failed}` + `longshort.combiner.book_published` (4th event added at 3.0a per ACT-232 R3b — Phase-4 portfolio-sizing trigger surface, decoupled from `compute.completed` so consumers can subscribe to book finalization independently; cheap to add now, expensive to retrofit once Phase-4 consumers wire to the compute event; register in `event-index.md` at the 3.0a build PR). (8) MIG-099 LANDED at ACT-233 (3.0a schema apply) carrying schema + RLS + grants + indexes; ledgered same-commit. (9) Reference indexes updated same-PR (function-index, event-index incl. `longshort.combiner.book_published`, route-index N/A — no new routes). (10) `signal_registry.composite` row STAYS `status='planned'` at 3.0a per disposition (b); flips to `live` at 3.3 trained-model promotion. |

 | **Schema invariants (verbatim — load-bearing)** | (a) `combiner_rankings.ranker_source` is `text NOT NULL`; at 3.0 EVERY row carries literal `'count_normalized_fallback'`. (b) `combiner_model_registry` has a partial unique index `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX combiner_model_registry_one_active_per_side ON combiner_model_registry (side) WHERE status='active'`. (c) At 3.0 no row in `combiner_model_registry` has `status='active'` (degraded-path attestation surface). (d) `combiner_book` has `UNIQUE(operator_id, as_of_date, side, rank_within_side)` preventing duplicate sized positions. (f) **At 3.0b NO `Decimal('-999')` is written to `combiner_feature_vectors.features` — `__is_present=0` carries absence per §2/L387 axiom 3 and §6.5.3 "all other code paths use `Optional[Decimal]`". The `Decimal('-999')` introduction site is repositioned to the 3.2 in-process model-input construction function (immediately before LightGBM `.predict()`; exact path resolved at 3.2 build PR) per ADR-008a (supersedes ADR-008, ACT-234). The `scripts/check-sentinel-patterns.ts` override annotation (literal `// allow-sentinel-fallback: ADR-008` matching scanner regex `/\/\/\s*allow-sentinel-fallback:\s*ADR-\d+/`) + `docs/banned-patterns.md` registry entry + `check-sentinel-patterns_test.ts` single-site pinning all land at the 3.2 build PR, NOT at 3.0b.** |
 | **Exit-gate assertion (queryable; binding)** | The 3.0 build is CLOSED only when BOTH of the following return zero rows against live DB: (1) `SELECT 1 FROM combiner_model_registry WHERE status='active'` (no trained-model promoted at 3.0); (2) `SELECT 1 FROM combiner_rankings WHERE ranker_source <> 'count_normalized_fallback'` (every ranking row is degraded-path). The 3.3 promotion FP MUST flip both queries to non-zero atomically (model insert with `status='active'` + first ranking row with `ranker_source='lambdarank_v1'` or successor) — giving 3.3 a clean before/after diff. |

(e) **Dual-date axis blind spot (the highest-stakes finding — newly surfaced by Phase 0 probes):** the original code keyed decay AND look-ahead on `transaction_date` only (grep: `compute-insider.ts:34` `age_days = (as_of − transaction_date) in days`; `compute-insider.ts:157,160,206` `ageDays(r.transaction_date!, as_of)`; `polygon-form4-fetcher.ts:91` `Used for decay (age_days = as_of − transaction_date)`). **There is no `acceptance_datetime` field in the persisted contract, and no look-ahead gate against acceptance.** Per Phase-0 lag table below, transactions become knowable up to ~5.95 calendar days AFTER `transaction_date` (the SEC 2-business-day filing rule spans weekends). An `as_of` computed at noon ET with `transaction_date` filtered to `[as_of−90d, as_of]` would have included transactions whose Form-4 had not been ACCEPTED yet at `as_of` — a silent look-ahead bias of up to ~5 calendar days for filings near the window-right boundary. The phantom-firehose bug (a) made this latent rather than active (the compute never saw real transactions), but the rebuild MUST close this axis at v1 — see DEC-058 §(b). |

Alternative `index.json` exists at same path; not used at Phase 0. |

#### §B2 — Raw probe evidence (UA discipline + CIK coverage + filing shape + Form-4 XML samples + dual-date lag table)

**Fair-access pre-flight (DEC-058 §(g) evidence).** SEC EDGAR fair-access policy requires a declared User-Agent identifying the requestor + contact; courtesy-caps ~10 req/s. Verified empirically:

```
# Probe 1: company_tickers.json WITHOUT UA → expected 403
curl -s -o /dev/null -w "HTTP=%{http_code} bytes=%{size_download}\n" \
  "https://www.sec.gov/files/company_tickers.json"
→ HTTP=403 bytes=1925

# Probe 2: company_tickers.json WITH UA → 200
curl -s -H "User-Agent: Lovable-Crosswind-Probe FP-050/0.1 (contact: ops@crosswind.invalid)" \
  -w "HTTP=%{http_code} bytes=%{size_download}\n" \
  "https://www.sec.gov/files/company_tickers.json"
→ HTTP=200 bytes=796479
→ TOTAL_ROWS=10416
→ sample: NVDA(CIK 1045810), GOOGL(CIK 1652044), AAPL(CIK 320193)
```

**Form-4 XML field-to-compute-contract map (DEC-058 §(a)-(e) inputs).**

| Compute need (§4.4.4) | Form-4 XML XPath | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `transaction_code` (P, S, M, A, C, G, F, ...) | `nonDerivativeTable/nonDerivativeTransaction/transactionCoding/transactionCode` | IN-set per §(a) binding. |
| `transaction_date` (decay anchor) | `nonDerivativeTable/nonDerivativeTransaction/transactionDate/value` | Original (failed) build's decay key — preserved as decay axis per §(b). |
| `transaction_shares` | `transactionAmounts/transactionShares/value` | |
| `transaction_price_per_share` | `transactionAmounts/transactionPricePerShare/value` | `0` for code M/A (no cash price). |
| `acquired_disposed` (sign) | `transactionAmounts/transactionAcquiredDisposedCode/value` | A=acquired, D=disposed. |
| `direct_or_indirect_ownership` | `ownershipNature/directOrIndirectOwnership/value` | Per §4.4.4 not weighted; recorded for audit. |
| `is_officer` / `is_director` / `is_ten_percent_owner` | `reportingOwner/reportingOwnerRelationship/{isOfficer,isDirector,isTenPercentOwner}` | Role-weight tier inputs per §4.4.4 + DEC-044. |
| `officer_title` (NEO-heuristic input) | `reportingOwner/reportingOwnerRelationship/officerTitle` | DEC-044 `classifyRoleWeight` consumes this. |
| `10b5_1_indicator` | Free-text scan for `/10b5[- ]?1/i` in the XML body (footnote text) | Form 4 has NO structured 10b5-1 flag; check-box at form level + footnote prose. Phase 0 evidence: 5/5 AMZN filings carried the mention → DEC-058 §(c) detector design question. |
| `issuer_cik` | `issuer/issuerCik` | Cross-check with `company_tickers.json` lookup. |
| `accession_number` | (URL path) | Idempotency key — see DEC-058 §(h). |
| `acceptance_datetime` | NOT in XML — from `submissions/CIK{cik}.json` `filings.recent.acceptanceDateTime[i]` | **The dual-date axis — DEC-058 §(b) binding.** |

**THE DUAL-DATE AXIS — lag distribution table (10 filings; 9 with non-derivative tx; 1 derivative-only).**

| CIK | Accession | `transactionDate` (min) | `periodOfReport` | `acceptanceDateTime` | Lag (cal days) | Codes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1018724 | 0001018724-26-000020 | 2026-05-21 | 2026-05-21 | 2026-05-26T22:33:10Z | **5.94** | S, M |
| 1018724 | 0001936006-26-000018 | 2026-06-01 | 2026-06-01 | 2026-06-03T20:27:07Z | **2.85** | S |
| 1018724 | 0001374545-26-000008 | 2026-05-21 | 2026-05-21 | 2026-05-26T22:47:08Z | **5.95** | M, S |
| 1018724 | 0002024813-26-000008 | 2026-05-21 | 2026-05-21 | 2026-05-26T22:39:49Z | **5.94** | S, M |
| 1018724 | 0001557979-26-000006 | 2026-05-21 | 2026-05-21 | 2026-05-26T22:26:07Z | **5.93** | S, M |
| 5272    | 0002025132-26-000006 | 2026-06-03 | 2026-06-03 | 2026-06-03T21:05:52Z | **0.88** | F |
| 5272    | 0002076280-26-000005 | (none — derivative-only) | 2026-06-01 | 2026-06-01T20:58:59Z | n/a | (no ND) |
| 1467623 | 0002060166-26-000012 | 2026-06-01 | 2026-06-01 | 2026-06-03T20:47:24Z | **2.87** | S, A |
| 63276   | 0001967445-26-000004 | 2026-05-29 | 2026-05-29 | 2026-06-02T21:30:45Z | **4.90** | M, F |
| 1037646 | 0001037646-26-000038 | 2026-05-12 | 2026-05-12 | 2026-05-14T14:48:04Z | **2.62** | A |

**Lag stats (n=9 with ND tx):** min=0.88d, max=5.95d, median≈3d, mean≈4.2d. **All filings spec-compliant in business-day terms** (the 5.93-5.95d cluster spans Thu→Tue = 2 business days). **Implication:** an `as_of` at noon ET with `transaction_date ∈ [as_of−90d, as_of]` includes transactions whose Form-4 was not yet accepted at `as_of` — confirmed look-ahead bias up to ~6 calendar days. This is the failure mode the ORIGINAL build's `compute-insider.ts:34,157,160,206` + `polygon-form4-fetcher.ts:91` blind to. DEC-058 §(b) closes it.

**Architecture-fork arithmetic (per-CIK vs daily-feed; DEC-058 §(i) binding).**

| Branch | Per-fire cost (incremental, daily after-close cadence) | Per-fire cost (90-day backfill) | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Per-CIK** (839 universe × 1 submissions.json) | 839 reqs @ ≤5 rps self-imposed → ~168 s wall + per-match XML fetch (Phase-0 AMZN: 526 Form-4 in `recent` → most names will have ≪10 Form-4 in last 24h). Estimate: 839 + ~30 XML ≈ 174 s. | 839 + ~7,500 XML ≈ 1,667 s ≈ 28 min sequential. Backfill is one-shot. | Authoritative per-CIK; no client-side filtering of an out-of-universe firehose. CIK-mapping quirks per §(f). |
| **Daily-feed** (1 daily form.idx × 1-N days) | 1 idx (~0.9 MB) + filter to in-universe CIKs (~5% of 1,667 ≈ 83) + 83 XML fetches @ 5 rps ≈ 17 s + 1 s idx ≈ 18 s. | 90 idx + ~7,500 XML ≈ 1,500 s ≈ 25 min. | One file/day; deterministic shape. **Strongly favors incremental.** Per-CIK option still useful for ad-hoc backfill of a single name. |

**Phase-0 leaning:** daily-feed primary for incremental; per-CIK retained as a focused-lookup tool. Both bounds within the 600 s `job_registry.timeout_seconds`. The FP-045 queue-engine availability is noted but NOT obviously needed at v1 wall-clock estimates — DEC-058 §(i) binding question is whether the daily-feed primary is single-invocation or queue-paged.

#### §B3 — DEC-058 skeleton (every binding is a QUESTION for operator ratification; UNRATIFIED)

| Binding | Question for operator | Failure mode addressed | Phase-0 evidence anchor |
|---|---|---|---|
| **§(a) Transaction-code IN-set** | Propose: **IN = {P (open-market purchase), S (open-market sale)}** at v1, exactly per §4.4.4 verbatim. **OUT = {M (option exercise), C (conversion), A (RSU vest / award), G (gift), F (tax-withheld), I (discretionary)}**, also per §4.4.4 verbatim. **Named deviation:** v1 reads §4.4.4 strictly — exercise-and-sell sequences (the AMZN cluster of M-then-S) contribute ONLY the S leg, NOT the option exercise; this matches §4.4.4 but operator should confirm whether the M-leg's strike-vs-market spread carries information that warrants a future enhancement-FP. | INC-70 phantom signal (the original "code filter" was a client-side post-firehose filter against a wrong data set; the rebuild's code filter is applied against authoritative per-CIK data, so the IN-set actually means something). | All 5 AMZN Form-4 samples in §B2 — every filing carries M+S pairs, no pure P; the M-leg is OUT per §4.4.4, S-leg is IN iff not 10b5-1. |
| **§(b) Dual-date axis (HIGHEST-STAKES)** | Two options. **Option A (conservative; proposed):** persist BOTH `transaction_date` AND `acceptance_datetime` per row; the look-ahead gate keys on `acceptance_datetime ≤ as_of` (what was knowable); decay age uses `transaction_date` (per spec §4.4.4 `age_days = (as_of − transaction_date)`). **Option B (simpler v1; operator approximation):** acceptance-gate AND decay both keyed on `acceptance_datetime` — named approximation that shifts decay anchor by the lag distribution (mean ~4.2d, max ~6d) and over-decays transactions by up to ~30% of a half-life. Either option closes the look-ahead blind spot the original build silently violated. | **The dual-date blind spot newly surfaced at Phase 0 history-recovery** — `compute-insider.ts:34,157,160,206` + `polygon-form4-fetcher.ts:91` keyed everything on `transaction_date` only; no `acceptance_datetime` in the persisted contract. | §B2 dual-date lag table (n=9): min 0.88d, max 5.95d, median ≈3d. |
| **§(c) 10b5-1 plan transactions** | Propose **EXCLUDE** all `S`-coded transactions on filings carrying any 10b5-1 mention (the §4.4.4 "discretionary sales, excluding 10b5-1" clause). Detector: free-text scan for `/10b5[- ]?1/i` in the Form-4 XML body (Form 4 has no structured 10b5-1 flag at the per-transaction level — the indication is a form-level checkbox + free-text footnote prose). **Reliability concern:** the AMZN sample showed 5/5 filings with the mention, suggesting either (a) AMZN executives universally trade via 10b5-1 (plausible; Big-Tech disclosure norm), or (b) the mention surfaces even when only SOME tx rows are 10b5-1-covered (would over-exclude). Operator confirm the conservative-over-exclusion stance for v1, with a footnote-position upgrade logged as a DW if Phase-7 IC ablation shows S-exclusion drags signal. | Spec compliance — §4.4.4 explicit exclusion. Implicit: 10b5-1 sales are pre-scheduled and structurally carry no insider-information edge. | All 5 AMZN samples in §B2 — all carry `10b5-1 mention: True`. |
| **§(d) Role weighting** | Spec §4.4.4 verbatim: **CEO/CFO 1.0; NEOs 0.7; Section 16 officers 0.4; independent directors 0.3; 10%+ owners 0.5.** FP-042 already implemented this via DEC-044's title-heuristic `classifyRoleWeight` (NEO=0.7 approximated by title-string classifier; DEF-14A authoritative enrichment deferred to DW-093). **Reuse as-is** per ACT-156 contract preservation. Operator confirm no new role-weight bindings at v1. | Contract preservation (ACT-156: FP-042 compute / classifier / filter / z-score is correct and reused as-is). DW-093 unchanged. | §B2 AMZN samples carry distinct `officerTitle` strings: "CEO Worldwide Amazon Stores", "President and CEO", "CEO Amazon Web Services", "Senior Vice President", "Senior Vice President" — DEC-044's heuristic table consumes these unchanged. |
| **§(e) Formula / size semantics** | Spec §4.4.4 verbatim: `raw_signal_N = sum_over_90d(shares × price × sign × role_weight × exp(-age_days / 14)) / market_cap`. Where `shares × price` = dollar value, `sign = +1 for A (acquired) on code P, −1 for D (disposed) on code S` (after §(a) IN-set filter). Existing FP-042 `compute-insider.ts` implements this contract; **reuse as-is** per ACT-156. | Contract preservation; INC-70 left compute correct, only data layer wrong. | n/a (formula-level; spec-cited). |
| **§(f) CIK-mapping maintenance + unmapped-name handling** | Two sub-bindings. **(f1) Refresh cadence:** `company_tickers.json` snapshot refreshed at universe-refresh cadence (quarterly per current plan) OR on every fire (10,416-row 800 KB fetch ≤1 s — negligible). Propose: **fetch-per-fire** for staleness safety. **(f2) Mapping override table:** for ticker conflicts (Phase-0 evidence: `NXT` resolves to Nextpower CIK 1852131 but S&P-500 NXT is Nextracker CIK 1953967), maintain a small operator-curated override map in code (`INSIDER_CIK_OVERRIDES: Record<ticker, cik>`). Unmapped names emit typed `skip='ticker_to_cik_unresolved'` per the FP-042 typed-skip discipline — never silent. | INC-70 made silent ticker→data mismatch the failure shape; we close it with explicit typed skip when ticker can't resolve. | §B2 CIK coverage block — 15/15 resolved BUT one mapping conflict (NXT) found. |
| **§(g) UA + self-imposed RPS cap** | Spec the UA header constant in the fetcher: `User-Agent: Lovable-Crosswind/{module_version} (contact: <ops_email>)` — operator provides `<ops_email>` as a new secret `EDGAR_CONTACT_EMAIL` (NOT a credential; an SEC fair-access identifier). **Self-imposed RPS cap: 5 rps** (half SEC's published 10 rps headroom — DEC-034 chokepoint discipline). Pre-flight check at fetcher construction: a no-UA probe MUST return 403 (proves we're talking to the right endpoint with the right policy); a with-UA probe MUST return 200. | Phase-0 evidence: no-UA returns 403; with-UA returns 200. Without this binding the fetcher fails 100% silently the first time it's invoked. | §B2 fair-access pre-flight block. |
| **§(h) Form 4/A amendment policy** | Form 4/A = amendment to a prior Form 4 (corrects errors). Daily-index 2026-06-11 evidence: 6 Form-4/A vs 1,667 Form-4 → ~0.36% rate. Propose: **treat 4/A identically to 4** at v1 (same XML schema; the amendment supersedes the original transaction record by accession number). Idempotency: persist per row `idempotency_key = (issuer_cik, accession_number, transaction_seq)`; on Form 4/A re-fetch, the new accession produces new rows that ON-CONFLICT-DO-NOTHING; the original Form 4's rows remain in the table (audit preserved) and the compute layer should prefer the most-recent accession for the same (issuer_cik, owner_cik, transaction_date) tuple — operator confirm or counter-propose. | Idempotency requirement (Constitution / FP-045 precedent). Original FP-042 had no amendment policy because the phantom firehose made it moot. | §B2 daily-index 6 4/A out of 1,667 → low but non-zero rate. |
| **§(i) Architecture branch (per-CIK vs daily-feed)** | Per §B2 arithmetic: daily-feed primary (~18 s/fire incremental, ~25 min backfill) vs per-CIK (~174 s/fire incremental, ~28 min backfill). Both fit `job_registry.timeout_seconds=600` at incremental cadence; backfill is one-shot via manual handler. Propose: **daily-feed primary + per-CIK fallback** for focused lookups, single-invocation (NOT queue) — both bounds within FP-047 single-invocation envelope. If a future cadence change to intraday-15-min surfaces, revisit FP-045 queue. | INC-70's "per-ticker fan-out across a non-authoritative firehose" was the worst of both worlds; daily-feed gives us a single authoritative index. | §B2 architecture-fork arithmetic table. |
| **§(j) Cross-signal consistency with DEC-057 §(c)** | DEC-057 §(c) ratified ADDITIVE independence: §4.4.9 Tier-2 "significant insider transaction" overlaps Signal #4 (`insider_transactions_90d`); both signals score the same event independently by design (catalyst measures decayed event-presence; insider measures dollar-weighted magnitude). **Record consistency requirement:** any change to Signal #4's IN-set per §(a) MUST NOT silently change the catalyst classifier's "significant insider transaction" Tier-2 detection (currently keyword-derived per DEC-057 §(g); structurally orthogonal — confirmed). | Double-count prevention by explicit design choice rather than silent gating. | DEC-057 §(c) text + Signal #9's catalyst keyword fetcher's structural separation from Form-4 XML pipeline. |

| **Spec — §4.4.9 verbatim (`docs/04-modules/longshort/design-source/CROSSWIND_SPEC.md:546-567` — grep-verified)** | <pre>For each catalyst event E for name N in trailing 5 trading days:<br>  catalyst_weight(E) = 3.0 (Tier 1) \| 1.5 (Tier 2) \| 0.5 (Tier 3)<br>  age_weight(E) = exp(-age_in_hours / catalyst_specific_half_life)<br>raw_signal_N = sum(catalyst_weight(E) × age_weight(E))</pre> **Tier 1 (3.0):** Earnings; M&A; FDA approval/rejection/advisory; regulatory action; major guidance update; CEO/CFO change. **Tier 2 (1.5):** Major analyst rating/coverage; material partnership/contract; buyback/dividend change; significant insider transaction; stock split/special dividend. **Tier 3 (0.5):** Minor analyst rating change; conference; non-material product launch; investor day. **Catalyst-specific half-lives (spec-stated):** Earnings 48h; M&A 96h; FDA 72h; Regulatory 96h; Guidance 48h; Executive change 72h; Analyst change 24h; Other Tier 2/3 24–48h. **Active window:** 5 trading days lookback. **Sign:** Unsigned (positive only — direction captured by other signals). **Data source line (verbatim):** "Polygon News + Polygon Reference + SEC EDGAR primary; Benzinga direct, Tradier corporate actions backup." **Missing-data (§4.3.5 non-critical):** `None` when no catalyst events in window → `(value=Decimal('-999'), is_present=0)` to combiner. **Normalization:** within-sector GICS z-score, clipped at ±3. **Cadence (spec):** 5 min during market hours; 15 min after-hours. |
| **Registry truth (grep-verified at HEAD)** | `signal_registry` row seeded `planned` at MIG-076 (`supabase/migrations/20260608152448_71f5dd70-d2ba-4380-b8f8-7d97af1196ee.sql:96-98`): `signal_id='active_catalyst_flag'`, `signal_number=9`, `name='Active catalyst flag'`, `spec='§4.4.9'`, `cadence='intraday (5 min)'`, `status='planned'`, `criticality='non_critical'`, `job_registry_id=NULL`, `planned_phase='Phase 2.9'`, `display_order=9`. **No `job_registry` seed exists for Signal #9** (consistent with the other planned signals at this stage; the row is created at Phase-3 wiring per the FP-047/048 precedent — not a finding). The exact `signal_id` for downstream code is `active_catalyst_flag`. |
| **Architecture branch (NOT YET SELECTED — DEC-057 binding)** | Single-invocation orchestrator for the catalyst-detection layer is plausible: per-call families are all small (calendars ~hundreds to low-thousands of rows trailing-5-days; news single-page ≤1000 items × ~2 pages for a 5-day window per §4.4.8 measured shape) and the per-name z-score normalization is panel-shaped. The branch decision is deferred to Phase 1 because the event-type → vendor binding (B1 table) determines whether the discovery layer is **N calendar-pulls (~6 endpoints, all paged trailing-5-days)** vs **per-symbol fan-out (839 × M endpoint calls)**. The current evidence strongly favors **Branch B+H ("calendars-plus-headline-keywords")**: 6 global calendar pulls (earnings, M&A, dividends, splits, IPO, FDA advisory) ≈ 6 calls × ~200–500ms = 1–3s wall; Polygon news with `insights[]` + `keywords[]` for keyword-derived tiers (CEO change, guidance, regulatory action, partnership) ≈ 2 pages × 6.3s wall ≈ 13s; FMP grades-latest-news feed (reusable from Signal #1) for ratings ≈ 4 calls × 100ms; total ≈ 20s under 150s budget. **Both bounds** (latency + rate) live in the Phase 1 build per FP-047 / FP-048 discipline. |

#### FP-049 §B1 — Event-type → endpoint mapping table (grep-and-probe-verified)

Polygon news keyword-derived M&A is **secondary** to dedup small-cap deals that may not surface on the SEC S-4-derived FMP feed. |

- `/press-releases?symbol=AAPL&from=&to=`: HTTP **403** — gated.

**Tradier (NOT PROBED this Phase 0)** — the spec data-source line names "Tradier corporate actions backup"; given the strong Polygon + FMP coverage of splits/dividends already evidenced above, Tradier is left as the explicit backup-tier with a DEC-057 §(i) binding to ratify (use only when primary returns `unavailable`).

**Coverage sample (5 names, trailing 5d earnings-occurred + trailing 5w dividends + trailing 5d news with keywords):**
- AAPL: 0 earnings-occurred (next 2026-07-29 forward); 1 ex-dividend 2026-05-11; 50+ news items 7d (insights+keywords populated 100 %).
- MSFT: 0 earnings-occurred (next 2026-07-29 forward); 0 dividends 5w; news coverage ample (FP-048 measured 92 items 7d).
- JPM: 0 earnings-occurred (next 2026-07-14 forward); news coverage moderate.
- JNJ: 0 earnings-occurred (next 2026-07-15 forward).
- KO: 0 earnings-occurred (next 2026-07-28 forward).
→ **None of the 5 sampled names had a trailing-5-day earnings event in this off-cycle slice** (typical for the week before earnings season). The §4.4.9 catalyst-density expectation is correctly low in off-cycle windows; the spec's `None`-when-empty semantic (§4.3.5 non-critical) handles this without fabricated zeros. **Reconciliation with FP-048's 11.4 % news coverage:** that figure was for `insights[]`-derived sentiment scoring; for §4.4.9 catalyst-keyword inference the relevant question is "does at least one news item with a catalyst-keyword appear in trailing 5d per name" — Phase-1 will measure this against the keyword taxonomy ratified in DEC-057 §(b)+§(j).

#### FP-049 §B3 — Gap table + DEC-057 binding skeleton (every binding enumerated for operator ratification)

| Binding | Spec text / question | Proposed v1 binding (NOT YET RATIFIED) | Rationale / honest tradeoff |
|---|---|---|---|
| **§(a) Half-life operational table** | Spec gives "Other Tier 2/3 24–48h" — a range, NOT a value | **Earnings 48h · M&A 96h · FDA 72h · Regulatory 96h · Guidance 48h · Executive change 72h · Analyst change 24h · Partnership 36h · Buyback/Dividend change 36h · Insider transaction 24h · Split/special-dividend 24h · Conference 24h · Product launch 36h · Investor day 36h** | Spec-stated values used verbatim where given (8 of 13). For "Other Tier 2/3" the proposed values pick the midpoint (36h) for genuinely multi-day relevance (partnership/buyback-announcement/launch/investor-day) and the low-end (24h) for single-event point-in-time signals (insider tx / split / conference). Operator may adjust each independently; recorded values become the `CATALYST_HALF_LIFE_HOURS` constant table referenced by the Phase-2 compute function. |
| **§(b) Tier-assignment authority + keyword-inference taxonomy** | Spec lists event types per tier but does NOT say which vendor is authoritative per type, nor a keyword taxonomy for inferred types | **Authority-per-type:** (1) Structured-endpoint = authoritative when available (earnings: FMP; M&A: FMP; ratings: FMP grades; splits/dividends: Polygon; FDA-advisory: Finnhub; corporate-actions backup: Tradier). (2) Keyword-derived secondary when structured is absent (CEO/CFO change, regulatory action, guidance, partnership, buyback announcement, FDA approval/rejection outcome). (3) **Minimum-keyword set per event family with action-verb gate** — published as the `CATALYST_KEYWORDS` lookup in Phase-1 fetcher; values land in DEC-057 §(b) appendix. **Named misclassification risk:** keyword-derived events have higher false-positive rate; the Tier-1/2/3 weighting partially absorbs this (false-positive on a Tier-3 keyword is 0.5 × decay = small contribution); Phase-7 IC ablation will measure whether keyword-derived events should be reweighted. | The action-verb gate is the single highest-impact false-positive control; e.g., "guidance" alone is not an event, but "raises guidance" / "lowers guidance" / "withdraws guidance" + a numeric description-field match is. |
| **§(c) Cross-signal independence flags** | Two §4.4.9 event types overlap with other §4.4.x signals: **Tier-2 "Major analyst rating"** overlaps Signal #1 (analyst_revision_drift, §4.4.5); **Tier-2 "Significant insider transaction"** overlaps Signal #4 (insider transactions, §4.4.4). Same per-event row would be consumed by two signals. | **Question for operator:** do §4.4.5 and §4.4.9 score the SAME analyst-rating row independently (additive double-count is real but acceptable because they measure different things — drift vs flag-presence), OR does #9's "Major analyst rating" event count be GATED off whatever rows #1 has already scored (anti-double-count)? Same question for #4 (insider sized-signal) vs #9 (insider event-occurred flag). **Proposed v1 binding:** INDEPENDENT scoring (additive). Rationale: §4.4.9 measures catalyst-presence (decayed event count, not magnitude); §4.4.5/§4.4.4 measure magnitude (per-event signed). They are orthogonal predictors and additive double-count is the correct combiner-input shape. Operator may override to gated/anti-double-count. | Recording the question is the deliverable; the chosen binding lands in DEC-057 §(c). |
| **§(d) Upcoming-vs-occurred earnings (the discovery axis)** | Spec says "For each catalyst event E for name N in trailing 5 trading days" → ACTIVE catalyst = event that OCCURRED in window. But FMP/Finnhub earnings calendars return BOTH past and future dates (probe evidence above). | **Proposed v1 binding:** OCCURRED-ONLY (forward earnings are NOT a §4.4.9 catalyst; PEAD's #2 covers post-event drift). The operational filter: `event_date ≤ as_of AND event_date ≥ as_of − 5_trading_days`. **However:** the spec is ambiguous on "upcoming-earnings-in-window" (the 1–2 day lead-in is a known pre-earnings drift driver). **Alternative for operator consideration:** include upcoming-within-2-trading-days as Tier-2 (half the weight; "anticipation catalyst"). Lands in DEC-057 §(d). | The Finnhub `hour` enrichment (`bmo`/`amc`) determines decay-origin precision for occurred earnings — set `event_at = date + 09:30 ET (bmo) / 16:00 ET (amc) / 12:00 ET (blank)`. |
| **§(e) Announcement-date vs ex-date (the decay-origin axis)** | For splits/dividend changes: the EVENT for §4.4.9 decay purposes is the **announcement** (`declarationDate` / `declaration_date`) — that's when news hits and price reacts. The ex-date is a separate technical event (price gap, not new info). Probe: FMP `declarationDate` is empty on many calendar rows; Polygon `declaration_date` is more consistently populated. | **Proposed v1 binding:** decay-origin = `declaration_date` (Polygon) for dividends/specials/buyback announcements; fall back to FMP `declarationDate` only when Polygon row absent for the ticker; emit typed skip `declaration_date_unavailable` (counted, not silently dropped) when both are empty rather than substituting ex-date (which would emit a phantom catalyst at the gap). Lands in DEC-057 §(e). | Buyback "announcements" lack a structured source entirely → keyword-derived from press-releases (anchor `published_utc`). |
| **§(f) 5-trading-day → calendar-day window arithmetic** | Spec says "trailing 5 trading days" — needs a calendar conversion rule + holiday handling. | **Proposed v1 binding:** trading-day calendar = NYSE business-day calendar (per `pandas_market_calendars` shape, but implemented as a calendar-aware date stepper over `signal_compute_log.as_of_date`); 5 trading days back ≈ 7 calendar days mid-week, 8–9 days across a weekend or holiday week. The orchestrator computes `window_start_at = nthPrecedingTradingDay(as_of, 5)` and emits both bounds in `signal_compute_log` metadata for replay determinism. Lands in DEC-057 §(f). | Aligns with §4.4.5 / §4.4.8's calendar-day windows; the 5-trading-day vs 7-calendar-day difference is small but matters for events landing on weekends (e.g., Friday-evening guidance announcement scored on Monday). |
| **§(g) v1 event-type subset under current entitlement (the §(k)-rider deviation)** | Some event types have no structured source at our four-vendor entitlement and would be entirely keyword-derived. | **Proposed v1 binding — IN at v1:** Earnings (FMP), M&A (FMP), Splits (Polygon), Dividends/declarations (Polygon), Ratings (FMP grades), FDA-advisory (Finnhub), Executive-change (FMP key-executives diff + news keyword), Guidance (news keyword + action-verb gate), Regulatory action (news keyword + entity gate), Partnership (news keyword). **OUT at v1 (DEC-057 §(g) named deviation):** FDA approval/rejection outcomes (no structured source; keyword-only too noisy without an entity-gate to drug names); Buyback announcements (no structured source; defer to v2 when keyword-derivation taxonomy proves stable); Conferences (Tier 3; high false-positive rate; defer); Non-material product launches (Tier 3; high false-positive rate; defer); Investor day (Tier 3; defer with conferences). **Spec compliance:** §(k)-rider names the OUT-set explicitly; the v1 signal computes against the IN-set; the OUT-set is a deferred-work entry, NOT a silent drop. | Operator may flip any OUT to IN with a keyword taxonomy update — the architecture supports incremental addition. |
| **§(h) Source-of-truth precedence + cross-vendor dedup** | The same event may appear on two vendors (e.g., Polygon news + FMP press-release for the same partnership). Need a dedup key. | **Proposed v1 binding:** dedup key = `(ticker, event_type, event_at[rounded to 1-hour bucket])`. First-occurrence-wins (the INC-74 dedupe discipline). Vendor precedence per event-type table above (structured > keyword-derived). Lands in DEC-057 §(h). | Cross-vendor news dedup beyond the §4.4.8 publisher set is a known hazard; the 1-hour bucket is conservative (an event hitting two wires within an hour collapses to one catalyst — correct semantics). |
| **§(i) Tradier corporate-actions backup binding** | Spec names Tradier as backup; not probed this Phase 0. | **Proposed v1 binding:** Tradier consulted ONLY when Polygon `/v3/reference/splits` OR `/v3/reference/dividends` returns `unavailable` for the as_of window (typed-error path). No primary Tradier consumption at v1. Phase-1 builds the Tradier fetcher behind the same shared-fetcher shape (unavailable path returns typed enum, never silent empty). Lands in DEC-057 §(i). | Keeps Tradier in the surface area as the spec requires without paying its latency cost on every fire. |
| **§(j) Minimum-keyword taxonomy per event family** | Spec mentions keyword inference only by implication; the taxonomy is operator-ratifiable. | **Proposed v1 binding (the IN-set from §(g) only):** see B1 table per-row "keywords" columns. Codified as `CATALYST_KEYWORDS: Record<EventType, ReadonlyArray<string>>` in Phase-1 fetcher; the action-verb subset (§(b)) is a SEPARATE map `CATALYST_VERB_GATE: Record<EventType, ReadonlyArray<string>>`. Both lookups land verbatim in `docs/04-modules/longshort/signals/active-catalyst-flag.md` for operator audit at Phase-1 commit. | The keyword list is the single highest-volatility binding (operator may refine after Phase-1 false-positive measurement); v1 starts conservative + narrow. |

#### FP-049 — Forbidden (this PR, re-stated for the record)

No Signal-#4 / EDGAR work this PR. No compute / fetcher / orchestrator builds for #9. | **Phase-0b addendum — Finnhub news cross-probe (live, 2026-06-11, 8 of 15 calls; $0 — existing Estimate-1 entitlement)** | **Motivation:** Phase-0 found Polygon's pool = 4 publishers, zero tier-1/2 — the §4.4.8 tier-weighting reduces to a near-constant under our entitlement. Before ratifying DEC-056 bindings, cross-probe Finnhub (the paid PEAD vendor) to determine whether its `/company-news` surface is materially richer (tier-1/2 presence, coverage density). **Probe scaffolding:** transient `supabase/functions/probe-fp048b-finnhub/index.ts` deployed for live access (Supabase secret `FINNHUB_API_KEY` reachable only from deployed runtime); deployed → 8 probe calls executed → **DELETED in this same PR** per investigation-only scaffolding discipline (FP-047 / FP-048 Phase-0 precedent). **Calls (8 of 15 budget):** 1 entitlement+shape (AAPL trailing 7d) + 6 publisher pool (AAPL, NVDA, JPM, CAT, NKE, MA trailing 7d) + 1 `/news-sentiment?symbol=AAPL`. **Raw evidence (verbatim from probe response):**<br>**(1) Entitlement + shape.** `GET /company-news?symbol=AAPL&from=2026-06-04&to=2026-06-11` → HTTP 200, 150068 bytes, **244 articles in 7d** (≈4× the Polygon `ticker=AAPL` count of 63). Per-article keys: `category, datetime, headline, id, image, related, source, summary, url`. **No per-article sentiment field** (`sentiment`, `sentiment_score`, `score` all absent — confirmed honestly). Sample item (1 of 3): `{category:"company", datetime:1781191380, headline:"Why Intel Stock Was Just Upgraded to Buy from Sell at BofA", id:140607045, related:"AAPL", source:"Yahoo", summary:"Intel stock is double-upgraded to Buy from Underperform at BofA Securities…", url:"https://finnhub.io/api/news?id=786e510f…"}`.<br>**(2) Publisher pool (6 symbols, 873 total articles trailing 7d):** `Yahoo` 677 (77.5 %), `Benzinga` 73 (8.4 %), `CNBC` 46 (5.3 %), `SeekingAlpha` 43 (4.9 %), `ChartMill` 33 (3.8 %), `Fintel` 1. **CNBC PRESENT — first tier-2 publisher seen at any entitlement we hold.** Tier-1 (Reuters/Bloomberg/WSJ/FT/Dow Jones) and remaining tier-2 (Forbes/Barron's/NYT) ABSENT in this probe slice. Press-release wires (GlobeNewswire / BusinessWire / PR Newswire / Accesswire) **NOT seen** in the 873-item Finnhub slice (vs 37 % of the Polygon feed) — Finnhub appears to pre-filter PR wires upstream.<br>**(3) `/news-sentiment?symbol=AAPL`:** HTTP **403 — `{"error":"You don't have access to this resource."}`** — premium-gated at our Estimate-1 entitlement. The pre-scored aggregate (buzz / bullish-bearish %) is NOT available without an entitlement upgrade. Finding, not failure.<br>**(4) Timestamp + dedup.** `datetime` is unix seconds (all sampled values in [1.78e9, 1.79e9] — verified integer-seconds range), precision sufficient for the §4.4.8 hourly `exp(−age_hours/24)` decay. **39 articles appear under ≥2 symbols** in the 873-item slice (cross-symbol dedup count = 39; e.g. id=140572134 appeared under AAPL+CAT+NKE; id=140582052 under AAPL+CAT+NKE). The `related` field on the per-symbol endpoint **returns ONLY the queried symbol** (single-ticker string, not a multi-ticker list) — so the article's full ticker-set is only recoverable by per-symbol enumeration + de-dup-by-id (NOT a per-(article,ticker) sentiment shape — sentiment-less in the first place). **The per-(article,ticker) sentiment shape that Polygon `insights[]` provides cleanly is absent from Finnhub.**<br>**(5) Press-release presence:** zero PR-wire publishers in the 873-item slice — `source` field would identify them by name (e.g. "GlobeNewswire") if present; Finnhub appears to source from media aggregators (Yahoo) and direct news rooms (CNBC, Benzinga, SeekingAlpha) only. PR exclusion is structurally unnecessary at this entitlement (but the deny-list pattern remains forward-compatible). |

| **Phase-0b recommendation (the deliverable — for operator decision)** | **Recommendation: Polygon — vendor-of-record for Signal #8 v1.** Rationale: the §4.4.8 formula REQUIRES per-(article,ticker) sentiment; Polygon delivers it at 100 % presence; Finnhub does not deliver any per-article sentiment at all. Choosing Finnhub for v1 would force **FinBERT-in-v1** (the spec's named v2 fallback pulled into v1 build cost): adds an LLM inference path to the compute, an additional ~150-200 MB model artifact, GPU/CPU cost per article, and an entirely new gate surface (model-version drift, model-output validation). That is a material build cost the spec only budgeted as a v2 fallback contingent on Polygon insights-coverage failure — and Polygon coverage at v1 entitlement is 100 %. Finnhub's coverage-density advantage (~4× more articles) and CNBC tier-2 presence are real but do NOT compensate for losing the sentiment primitive — within-sector z-score normalizes amplitude, so denser-but-sentiment-less is strictly worse than sparser-but-sentiment-present. **Hybrid (Finnhub pool + FinBERT scoring vs Polygon pre-scored) is technically buildable but rejected for v1** on the same FinBERT-pull-in-cost grounds. Hybrid is the natural v2 path IF Polygon insights coverage degrades or operator wants strength-scoring (the v2 LLM enhancement-arc). **Action for operator:** confirm Polygon-as-vendor (no DEC-056 reshape needed — existing bindings stand) OR override with Finnhub-as-vendor (forces DEC-056 §(a)+(g) reshape to specify FinBERT-in-v1 with model-version pin, model-card, drift-monitoring contract — material additional scope; recommend a separate FP-048b for that path if chosen). |

The over-budget shell for Branch B is the **FP-045 queue engine with page-batches as cursor units** (page numbers are the cursor; one slice = N pages; the queue serializes slices the same way it serializes per-symbol work). Cross-references row updated accordingly: FP-043 entry removed; FP-045 is the over-budget shell. The Resolution-Shape paragraph also stands corrected — read "FP-045 queue engine with page-cursor units" everywhere the original said "FP-043 chunked-coordinator/worker template". |
| **Problem** | Signal #8 (`news_sentiment_7d`) is the next planned Phase-2 non-news/non-catalyst signal — registry row seeded `planned` at MIG-076 (`supabase/migrations/20260608152448_71f5dd70-d2ba-4380-b8f8-7d97af1196ee.sql`, `signal_id='news_sentiment_7d'`, `signal_number=8`, `criticality=non_critical`, `planned_phase='Phase 2.5'`, `job_registry_id=NULL`). CROSSWIND §4.4.8 (`docs/04-modules/longshort/design-source/CROSSWIND_SPEC.md:522`, quoted verbatim below) specifies: `signal_N = Σ_{A in 7d} provider_sentiment(A) × source_weight(A) × exp(−age_hours/24)` with provider_sentiment ∈ [−1,+1], source_weights ∈ {1.0, 0.7, 0.4, 0.1} per a 4-tier publisher classification, press releases excluded, provider pre-classified sentiment (Polygon/Benzinga per spec) with FinBERT local fallback (LLM v2-deferred), within-sector GICS z-score, 24h exponential half-life, multi-ticker articles split per the §4.4.8 primary-vs-equal-split rule. The Phase-0 probe maps spec→vendor shape and surfaces the v1 conscious-approximation set for DEC-056 ratification.<br><br>**§4.4.8 verbatim (CROSSWIND_SPEC.md:522-541):** `For each article A about name N in trailing 7 days: sentiment_A = provider_sentiment(A) // -1 to +1; source_weight(A) = 1.0 (tier-1: Reuters, Bloomberg, WSJ, FT, Dow Jones) / 0.7 (tier-2: CNBC, Forbes, Barron's, NYT business) / 0.4 (tier-3: Yahoo Finance, MarketWatch, aggregators) / 0.1 (tier-4: blogs, regional, low-quality); age_weight(A) = exp(-age_in_hours / 24); raw_signal_N = sum(sentiment_A × source_weight(A) × age_weight(A))`. Source filtering: "Wire + major newspapers + business publications. Press releases excluded." Multi-ticker: "Full sentiment to primary subject ticker; partial weight (divided by mentioned tickers) when no primary tag." |
| **Phase-0 probe evidence (live Polygon, 2026-06-11, 25 calls total — well under 40-call budget)** | **Entitlement:** `GET /v2/reference/news?ticker=AAPL&limit=10` → HTTP 200, real data, 429 ms wall, 10 results, `next_url` present. NOT plan-gated at our current Polygon entitlement (key already in env as the bars vendor; `Authorization: Bearer` accepted via Supabase secret — local dev-sandbox `POLYGON_API_KEY` returns 401 "Unknown API Key" but that is a sandbox-key issue, not endpoint gating; the probe ran from the deployed `probe-fp048-news` edge function reading the real Supabase secret). **Filter-honesty pre-flight (verifyFilterHonored pattern; mandatory per `_pattern-vendor-fetcher-filter-honesty.md`):** `ticker=ZZZZZZZZ&limit=10` → 200 / `count=0` ✓; `published_utc.gte=2099-01-01&published_utc.lte=2099-01-02&limit=10` → 200 / `count=0` ✓ — BOTH axes pass; the documented filters are honored. **Per-item shape (verified fields on AAPL probe):** `id`, `title`, `author`, `description`, `publisher.{name, homepage_url, favicon_url, logo_url}`, `published_utc` (ISO-8601 to-the-minute), `tickers[]`, `keywords[]`, `insights[]` of `{ticker, sentiment, sentiment_reasoning}` — **insights[] carries per-(article,ticker) sentiment**: in the AAPL=2026-06-11 WWDC item, AAPL→neutral while GOOG/GOOGL→positive on the same article (the multi-ticker per-ticker shape the spec needs is present). **Sentiment value domain:** observed values across the 1000-item global probe are `{positive, neutral, negative, mixed}` — categorical strings, NOT numeric ∈ [−1,+1]; mapping to numeric is a DEC-056 binding and "mixed" is a 4th case the spec does not enumerate. **insights presence rate:** 1000/1000 articles in the trailing global feed have non-empty `insights[]` → **100 % presence at our entitlement** — the FinBERT local fallback (§4.4.8) is unnecessary at v1 and is deferred to v2 as a named deferral, NOT silently dropped. **Publisher distribution (global feed `?limit=1000&order=desc&sort=published_utc`, single page, 6.3 s wall):** `GlobeNewswire Inc.` 371 (37.1 %), `The Motley Fool` 307 (30.7 %), `Benzinga` 255 (25.5 %), `Investing.com` 67 (6.7 %). **CRITICAL FINDING — narrow publisher pool:** at our Polygon entitlement the news pool consists of FOUR publishers. NONE of the §4.4.8 tier-1 wires (Reuters, Bloomberg, WSJ, FT, Dow Jones) and NONE of the tier-2 names (CNBC, Forbes, Barron's, NYT) appear in the most-recent 1000 items; a 7-day window probe returned the same four-publisher set. **Press-release exclusion is straightforward:** GlobeNewswire is a press-release wire and the publisher field cleanly identifies it (and PR Newswire / BusinessWire / Accesswire if/when they appear) — exclusion is by `publisher.name`-against-deny-list, NOT by content classification. Excluding GlobeNewswire removes 37 % of feed volume; what remains (Motley Fool / Benzinga / Investing.com) is tier-2-through-tier-4 only under the spec table. **Pagination:** `next_url` returned on every non-empty page; `limit=1000` is the per-page max. **Global feed depth:** the 7-day-window probe returned a full `limit=1000` page with `next_url_present=true` — global feed shape is supported (no ticker filter required). **Coverage sample across cap strata (20 names trailing 7d via per-symbol `ticker={t}&published_utc.gte={d-7}&limit=1000`):** NVDA 204, MSFT 92, AAPL 63, JPM 12, KO 12, BA 10, COST 9, GE 8, V 7, WMT 7, CAT 7, PFE 7, XOM 6, HD 6, DIS 5, NKE 4, UNH 3, TMUS 3, ABBV 2, MA 1. **All 20 names have ≥1 article in 7d; insights present on 100 % of returned articles.** Median ~7 articles/7d for non-mega names; mega-caps have 60–200+. Implication: the 7-day window will produce a non-empty panel for the vast majority of S&P 900 universe names; sparse-coverage small-caps emit the typed skip `no_articles_in_window` (added to §(e) of DEC-056 below). **Calls consumed:** 1 (entitlement) + 2 (publishers global, two windows) + 20 (coverage) + 2 (filter-honesty) = 25 of 40. **Probe scaffolding:** transient `supabase/functions/probe-fp048-news/` edge function deployed for investigation only and removed in this same PR (investigation scaffolding, not signal code — per FP-047 Phase-0 precedent). |
| **Architecture branch (recorded from evidence — both bounds presented)** | **Branch B — global feed paged single-invocation.** `/v2/reference/news?limit=1000&order=desc&sort=published_utc&published_utc.gte={as_of-7d}` returns a global time-ordered feed; pagination via `next_url`. **Per-call arithmetic at observed shape:** one page = 1000 items ≈ 6.3 s wall (mostly server-side time, not client-side decode). **Volume estimate:** the global feed yielded 1000 items "right now" with `next_url_present=true` immediately — assuming articles publish at roughly steady rate, a 7-day window contains approximately 7 × `(items_per_day)` items; with current observed publishing density and at `limit=1000` per page, **a 7-day window requires an estimated 35–70 pages** (5k–10k items/day × 7 days ÷ 1000/page). Pre-flight wall: 35–70 × ~3-6 s = ~3-7 minutes worst case; the orchestrator must run as chunked-coordinator/worker per the FP-043 template OR be split across multiple invocations on the FP-045 queue-engine. Phase 1 picks single-invocation vs chunked based on measured throughput against the 150 s HTTP wall. **Both-bounds discipline (Catalog #39):** *latency-bound* — 70 pages × ~3 s sequential = 210 s > 150 s wall → chunking required worst-case; *rate-bound* — Polygon rate cap on the news endpoint is **operator-supplied dashboard number (not citable from plan docs; same gap as FMP 750/min in FP-047)** — the cron handler's `TokenBucket` will enforce whatever ceiling operator provides at Phase 3 arm-up. Both bounds are recorded; Phase 1 implementation may not assume either is non-binding without re-measuring against the cap. **Branch A — per-symbol** (`?ticker={t}&published_utc.gte={d-7}&limit=1000`): 839 calls/run × ~0.4 s = ~5.5 minutes single-invocation; same chunking requirement; **strictly more expensive than Branch B** because Branch A duplicates multi-ticker articles across each tagged symbol's call. **Branch B is the architecture-of-record** for this FP; Branch A retained only as the not-chosen branch with the arithmetic preserved. |

NOT silently dropped (axiom 3).</td></tr><tr><td>Universe coverage</td><td>20/20 sampled names had ≥1 article in 7d (median ~7 articles/name)</td><td>§(h) Sparse-coverage names emit `no_articles_in_window` typed skip; Phase-7 evidence will measure unrecovered fraction and whether floor/regime is needed (NOT pre-committed).</td></tr></table> |

| **Architecture row (REVISED 2026-06-10 — supersedes original "Branch A: `/stable/price-target-latest-news` global feed-paged single invocation")** | **Branch A+H ("history-augmented"): Phase 1 fetcher pair.** (1) `/stable/price-target-latest-news?page=N&limit=100` paged ~35-37 calls for event discovery (unchanged from original Branch A). Events with no recovered same-analyst prior emit typed skip `revision_prior_unavailable`, contribute zero to the panel, and are counted in observability (Phase-7 ablation will measure whether the unrecovered-fraction's IC contribution materially changes the signal, gating any future Option-3-style fallback DEC). Pre-flight arithmetic (Phase-3 will re-state with measured fetch counts): event discovery 37 calls + per-symbol history ≤839 calls/day worst-case (one per universe name) at 750/min cap → ≤1.2 min wall = WELL within the 150s × multi-invocation budget per the same chunked-coordinator/worker template if needed; sequential single-invocation likely sufficient at ≤120s, but the Phase-3 build picks single-invocation vs FP-043 chunked-template based on measured throughput. Queue engine (FP-045) still not used. |

### FP-046: ROI-Enhancement Roadmap Bundle (DEC-054) — Squeeze-Guard, Trend-Quality, Quality, Regime, IC-Diagnostic, Asymmetric Sizing, Drawdown-Scaling

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **ID** | FP-046 (next-free after FP-045; grep-verified at HEAD `32c16407` — no prior allocation). |
| **Status** | approved-roadmap (DEC-054, operator 2026-06-10). Documentation-only container FP — authorizes NO build. Each enumerated item below ships via its own operator-authorized build FP at its assigned phase. |
| **Problem** | Operator-initiated strategic review on 2026-06-10 surfaced the momentum-exhaustion / reversal-risk concern (longs already topped, shorts already bottomed) as a structural class question against the 9-signal stack. The review (operator concern → supervisor analysis grounded in Da/Gurun/Warachka FIP RFS 2014, Novy-Marx 2013, Daniel-Moskowitz 2016, Barroso-Santa Clara 2015, George-Hwang JF 2004, EFMA 2025 crowded-short evidence, S3 short-squeeze retracement profile → second-opinion review → supervisor reconciliation) concluded: the spec already neutralizes the concern substantially by construction (12-1 skip-month in #6; Signal #7 reversal as CRITICAL anti-exhaustion; 5 of 9 signals information-flow rather than price-trend; learned combiner interactions; §3.3e SI>25% short exclusion). FOUR evidence-backed gaps remain (squeeze-guard, trend-quality, quality, regime). THREE additional items (long-vs-short IC diagnostic, asymmetric sizing, drawdown-conditional gross-scaling) are recorded so downstream consumers are designed for them. |
| **Resolution shape (R-table — roadmap only, each item is its own future build FP)** | **R2 — Squeeze-guard short-book veto (P0, Phase 2.10, build FP required).** Extend §3.3e: short-book entry vetoed when `(SI > 20% of float) AND (days-to-cover > 5) AND (5-day return > 0)`. DTC = SI ÷ 20d ADV, computed from existing Signal #5 + bars data; additionally exposed as a combiner feature. Rationale: squeeze retracement profile (S3: 50-70% retracement within 30-60 days) makes mid-squeeze shorts the tail-loss case; crowdedness research (EFMA 2025) finds crowding subsumes raw SI. $0 vendor cost. Highest-priority because portfolio-survival, not marginal ROI. **R5 — Long-vs-short IC diagnostic (P0, Phase 7 ablation spec, spec-only).** Phase 7 ablation MUST produce a per-signal × per-side IC table (long-tail IC vs short-tail IC, all 9 signals). Motivation: structural long-bias suspicion in #4 (insider purchases informative; sales noise), #1 (downgrade stickiness), #8 (negative-news overreaction). Gates R6. **R1 — Trend-quality combiner features (P1, Phase 2.11, build FP required).** Two new combiner feature pairs computed from the SAME Polygon daily bars Signal #6 already uses: (a) information discreteness `ID = sign(formation return) × (%negative days − %positive days)` over the T-21 → T-252 formation window (Da/Gurun/Warachka FIP, RFS 2014: momentum +5.94% → −2.07% over 6mo from continuous → discrete info at equal cumulative return); (b) formation-period realized volatility (GRJMOM lineage). Signal #6 itself is NOT modified; the combiner learns the interaction. **Recorded risk:** FIP's documented horizon is 6 months vs the §6.2 10-trading-day labels; the effect may not survive the horizon mismatch — Phase 7 ablation is the arbiter and retirement is a fully acceptable outcome. $0 vendor cost. **R3 — Signal #10: quality / gross-profitability (P1, Phase 2.12, build FP required).** GP/A (Novy-Marx 2013) from FMP Premium fundamentals endpoints (already subscribed — $0 marginal). Slow-moving, annually-refreshed inputs; within-sector z; non-critical class; standard (value, is_present) combiner pair. Signal count moves to 10 — within the spec's ~12 pre-ablation ceiling (signal-count discipline preserved verbatim). **R4 — Market-state regime features (P2, folded into the Phase 3 combiner build spec).** 2-3 features: trailing market return (sign + magnitude), trailing market realized vol — letting the ranker learn momentum-crash-state downweighting (Daniel-Moskowitz lineage). Spec-only now; lands with the combiner FP. **R6 — Asymmetric book sizing (P2, Phase 4, CONDITIONAL on R5 evidence).** If R5 shows materially weaker short-side IC, re-derive long/short counts (currently 20/20) from realized signal quality. No pre-commitment to any number; the diagnostic decides. **R7 — Drawdown-conditional gross-exposure scaling (P2, Phase 4/5 build; DEC clause now so downstream consumers of combiner output are designed for it).** Momentum-crash mitigation per Barroso–Santa Clara lineage. Specific rule parameters EXPLICITLY NOT set here — calibrated at Phase 7, not hardcoded. The clause reserves the architectural slot: portfolio construction consumes a gross-scaling multiplier input (default 1.0 until the rule ships). |

### FP-045: Generalized Cursor-Drain Queue-Worker (DEC-047) for Rate-Capped Signals — PEAD Re-Shell + Signal #3 Revival

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **ID** | FP-045 (next-free after FP-044; grep-verified at HEAD — only prior mention was FP-044's "Out of Scope" forward-reference, no allocation collision). |
| **Status** | Phase 0 (governance intake — this entry + INC-72 + `ai-failure-modes.md` entries) drafted; Phases 1–4 staged per the operator-authorized phased prompt with a supervisor STOP between phases. |
| **Problem** | INC-72 surfaced the second instance of the rate-bound single-invocation synchronous fan-out anti-pattern: FP-044's `longshort-pead-compute` 504'd at the 150s HTTP wall on its first operator test-fire and its isolate was terminated at ~190s with **zero `signal_compute_log` rows, zero `signal_observations` rows, and zero application log lines** (execution `11a267bc-ee77-4d54-a0ac-1571749f98cb`, 2026-06-09 20:21 UTC). The PEAD orchestrator ships with a vendor-cap arithmetic defect in its header (`pLimitedMap` concurrency-5 claimed "≤10 req/sec... well under the cap" — 10 req/sec = 600/min vs Finnhub's 300/min ceiling; concurrency-based throttling is latency-dependent and unbounded). At the correctly paced 5 rps cap, 1,678 Finnhub calls take 335s (~395s at the operator-measured 4.25 rps effective) — exceeds the 150s HTTP wall outright, approaches/matches the 400s Pro background-worker budget. Same class as FP-043's pre-token-bucket Tradier path (caught pre-production); promoted now to a structural class by this second firing. The DEC-047 cursor-drain queue-worker is the operator-approved resolution shape; this FP implements it once, generalized for both known rate-capped consumers (PEAD on Finnhub 300/min + options-flow on Tradier 120/min — the latter un-parks Signal #3 per FP-043's park clause and closes DW-095). |
| **Resolution shape (per-phase)** | **Phase 0 (this PR scope):** INC-72 record; `ai-failure-modes.md` §12.10 entries (supervisor-side: Phase-2 fresh-clone verification checked purity/enums/guards but not vendor-cap arithmetic, detection one step late at empirical 504; executor-side: rate-comment asserting 600/min "well under" a 300/min cap, gates structurally cannot catch vendor-cap math); FP-045 entry; FP-044 Status forward-fix to `complete-pending-reshell`. Docs only; gate 4; commit; STOP. **Phase 1 — schema:** MIG-082 introduces `signal_queue_runs`/`signal_queue_cursor`/`signal_queue_staging`/`signal_queue_skips` (generalized per `signal_id` — one table set, NOT per-signal tables); RLS per established deny-write/service-role pattern; idempotent; staging TTL handled by the orphan-sweeper rather than a separate cron; §22.5.1 live-DB read. STOP. **Phase 2 — generic engine:** `_shared/longshort-signals/shared/queue-worker/{queue-config,slice-worker,finalizer,orphan-sweeper}.ts` + edge handlers `longshort-queue-init` / `longshort-queue-slice` (serialized across signals so vendor caps never stack) / `longshort-queue-sweeper`. REUSES `token-bucket.ts` (productionClock-routed), `pLimitedMap`, `signal-types.ts`, `persist-signal-compute-log.ts`. No cadence constants (DEC-048). Per-slice audit event = INC-72 instrumentation fix. Pre-flight arithmetic row (calls/slice × rate vs 150s wall) recorded in PR evidence as the new executor discipline. Gates 2+4+11. STOP. **Phase 3 — PEAD consumer (re-shell):** queue-config registration consuming FP-044's `FinnhubEpsEstimateFetcher` + `FinnhubEarningsFetcher` + `computePead` UNTOUCHED. Synchronous-orchestrator path retired from cron entry; FP-044 orchestrator rate-comment defect corrected in same commit (file retained for the per-ticker arm). MIG-083 job_registry rows (DISARMED; DEC-048 interim language; slot non-overlapping). `JOB_ID_TO_SIGNAL_ID` entry + drift sentinels. `function-index.md` + `event-index.md` + `pead.md` addendum + queue-worker module doc (DEC-047 lineage) SAME-PR. §22.5.1. Gates + pre-flight arithmetic. STOP — then operator test-fire of the PEAD queue path validates before Phase 4. **Phase 4 — Signal #3 revival:** queue-config registration consuming FP-043's `TradierOptionsChainFetcher` + `computeOptionsFlow` + per-ticker arm UNTOUCHED. `signal_registry` `options_flow` planned→live (truth-in-telemetry precedent); DW-095 closed (evidence: this FP); DEC-047 status → implemented; FP-043 closure addendum. MIG-084 + §22.5.1. Operator test-fire validates. Closes the rate-capped class. |
| **Scope (Phase 0 — this PR)** | Doc edits only: this FP-045 entry; INC-72 (incidental-findings.md); two §12.10 operational-log entries in `ai-failure-modes.md` (supervisor-side blind-spot + executor-side rate-math defect); FP-044 Status field forward-fix per §22.3(e) to `complete-pending-reshell (orchestration shell superseded by FP-045; compute/fetchers/handlers reused)`. No code; no migration; no reference-index registrations (those land in Phase 2/3 same-PR as the engine + consumer registrations per Rule 6). |
| **Out of Scope** | NO changes to `compute-pead.ts`, `compute-options-flow.ts`, any fetcher logic, `z-score-normalize`, `persist-signal-compute-log` row shape, or `SignalSkipReason` enum (the queue engine maps existing skips, does not invent new ones). NO cron ENABLE on the queue handlers (operator test-fire-then-restore per DEC-040 / DEC-043). NO Signal #1 (`analyst-revisions`) or Signal #8 (`news`) work — those are separate FPs against the same engine once landed. NO `waitUntil` reliance — the queue-worker's design intent is that every invocation completes well inside the 150s wall, removing background-execution variance from the attestation path. Root cause is a vendor-cap × call-count × wall-budget architectural mismatch (`pead-orchestrator.ts` ships with header rate-math claiming 600/min is "well under" Finnhub's 300/min cap), not a code-correctness defect in the compute or fetcher layers. | **Problem** | Phase 2.6 deliverable: Signal #2 (post-earnings announcement drift, 20-day decay) per CROSSWIND §4.4.6. The signal requires a per-quarter analyst-consensus row carrying `epsAvg`, `epsHigh`, `epsLow`, `numberAnalysts` for the JUST-REPORTED quarter — needed to compute SUE = `(actual − consensus) / σ_proxy` with the DEC-051 range-proxy denominator and the DEC-052 N≥2 floor. The pre-build vendor audit (ACT-160 / chat-labeled "ACT-164") confirmed FMP `/stable/analyst-estimates?period=quarter` returns ONLY future-quarter rows — every already-reported quarter is gone from the endpoint by the time SUE is computed, so DEC-051 and DEC-052 cannot be satisfied on FMP alone. The earlier DEC-049 single-vendor framing is therefore superseded by DEC-053 (split-vendor: FMP for Signal #1, Finnhub for Signal #2). |
| **Resolution (Phase 1 ONLY in this PR)** | (a) NEW `FinnhubEpsEstimateFetcher` (`_shared/longshort-signals/shared/finnhub-eps-estimate-fetcher.ts`) wrapping `https://finnhub.io/api/v1/stock/eps-estimate?freq=quarterly`. Entitlement-aware (401/403 → `subscription_gated`, 404/empty → `data_unavailable`). Anti-phantom: drops any row missing `epsAvg` / `epsHigh` / `epsLow` / `numberAnalysts`; drops `numberAnalysts<=0` (vendor regression vs zero-coverage 404 distinction). Returns `{period, epsAvg, epsHigh, epsLow, numberAnalysts}` ASC-sorted; consumer filters as_of-relative downstream (no client-side filter in the fetcher — preserves replay-safety). Embedded `verifyFilterHonored` + `verifyFieldsPresent` static helpers. (b) NEW `FinnhubEarningsFetcher` (`_shared/longshort-signals/shared/finnhub-earnings-fetcher.ts`) wrapping `https://finnhub.io/api/v1/stock/earnings` — BARE ARRAY response with defensive type-guard (not a silent cast — a future wrapped-shape vendor regression would otherwise silently empty results). Returns `{period, actual, estimate}` with `actual` typed-null for future quarters. Same status mapping + dual-axis helpers. (c) Pacing decision (recorded in fetcher header): Finnhub 300/min ≫ 839 names; `pLimitedMap` bounded-concurrency only. NO token-bucket — deliberate contrast with FP-043's Tradier path where 120/min was the binding constraint. (d) Dual-axis verify + LOOK-AHEAD GATE re-run live with the actual fetcher classes — operational-window verdict 0/13 drifted → CLEAN; deep-history >4yr drift (13/392) recorded as observability finding, OUTSIDE the §4.4.6 stale-earnings cutoff so it cannot reach production SUE compute. |
| **Resolution (Phase 2 — staged; NOT in this PR)** | NEW pure `compute-pead.ts` (SUE × `exp(−trading_days_since_earnings / 20)`; typed skips `stale_earnings` / `insufficient_analysts` / `zero_dispersion` / `no_earnings_data` / `fetch_error`; reuses `shared/trading-days.ts` for the trading-day calendar). ### FP-043: Signal #3 — Options Flow Imbalance (5-day, smart-money filtered)

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **ID** | FP-043 (next-free after FP-042; grep-verified at HEAD). |
| **Status** | implemented (Phase 1 Tradier fetcher with dual-axis self-checks; Phase 2 compute + orchestrator; Phase 3 chunked coordinator/worker + token-bucket pacer + cron + manual handlers; Phase 4 MIG-078 disarmed `job_registry` seed + `signal_registry` planned→live flip + full doc set). Cron wiring + enable-flip pending separate operator-run DEC-043 attestation. |
| **Problem** | Phase 2.7 deliverable: Signal #3 (options flow imbalance, 5-day, smart-money filtered) per CROSSWIND §4.4.7 is the highest-cost signal in the stack ("~30-50% of daily compute budget"). It introduces a new vendor (Tradier production), the first chunked coordinator/worker architecture under a strict 120 req/min rate cap, and the first signal subject to a dual-axis fetcher discipline (filter-honored + fields-present). |
| **Resolution** | (a) NEW production Tradier fetcher (`tradier-options-chain-fetcher.ts`) wired against `api.tradier.com/v1`, entitlement-aware (401/403 → `subscription_gated`, 404/empty → `data_unavailable`), with embedded `verifyFilterHonored()` + `verifyFieldsPresent()` self-checks (the latter is INC-71's lesson). Tradier-normalisation helper handles the single-result-as-bare-object quirk. (b) NEW pure compute (`compute-options-flow.ts`) — 4-case direction classifier (call-buy-at-ask=+1, put-buy-at-ask=−1, call-sell-at-bid=−1, put-sell-at-bid=+1; sign error inverts the signal — all 4 cases + boundary equalities pinned by tests); smart-money filter (`volume>=100`, `DTE>=7`, `|delta|<=0.65`); 48-hour-half-life exponential decay keyed off `as_of` (no wall-clock); `MIN_QUALIFYING_PRINTS=5` floor; div-by-zero guard. (c) NEW orchestrator + chunk-runner + coordinator: shards the universe into N=6 strides, fans out HTTPS to N parallel workers, each worker paced by a shared token bucket at ~0.28 req/sec (≈ 100 req/min total = 6 × 0.28; ~15% headroom under the 120/min cap; absorbs 429 retries via the fetcher's [1s,2s,4s] backoff). Partial-failure honesty: a failed worker emits `fetch_error` skips for every ticker in its shard rather than silently dropping data (the Signal #4 lesson). (d) NEW `longshort-options-flow-worker` (CRON_SECRET, returns per-shard signals+skips); `longshort-options-flow-compute` (cron coordinator); `longshort-options-flow-compute-manual` (POST + JWT + `longshort.manage`, with `as_of` parsing + future-date guard). (e) Extended `SignalSkipReason` with `no_qualifying_flow` + same-commit `aggregateSkipCounts` seeding + `persist-signal-compute-log_test.ts` exact-match assertion update (FP-041 stale-test lesson applied). (f) Extended `JOB_ID_TO_SIGNAL_ID` + drift sentinel (cross-references `options-flow-orchestrator.ts::SIGNAL_ID`). (g) MIG-078 disarmed `job_registry` seed (schedule `'0 22 * * 1-5'`) + `signal_registry` planned→live flip (status='live', job_registry_id, stale_after_hours=72). signal_id locked: `'options_flow_imbalance_5d'`. |
| **Scope** | NEW `_shared/longshort-signals/shared/tradier-options-chain-fetcher.ts` (+ `_test.ts` 27 tests). NEW `_shared/longshort-signals/options-flow/{compute-options-flow,options-flow-orchestrator,options-flow-chunk-runner,options-flow-coordinator,token-bucket}.ts` (+ tests; 40+ total). NEW `supabase/functions/longshort-options-flow-{worker,compute,compute-manual}/{index.ts,index_test.ts}`. NEW MIG-078 (`job_registry` INSERT disarmed + `signal_registry` UPDATE planned→live).
